Continuing work from thread T-019b2794-20a6-703c-b2b4-621b5f1c832f. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@docs/feature/workspace-management.md @src/cli.rs @src/runtime/app.rs @src/messages.rs @src/update/layout.rs @src/model/editor_area.rs @src/model/mod.rs

Other relevant files: docs/ROADMAP.md

- I just completed splitter drag improvements for the split view system: fixed a potential clamp panic, eliminated per-frame allocations, added defensive bounds checking, and extracted a shared traversal helper in src/update/layout.rs.

- The editor is at v0.3.6 with CSV Cell Editing (Phase 2) completed. The codebase follows an Elm-like architecture with messages, update handlers, and commands.

- Key patterns in the codebase:
  - Messages are defined in src/messages.rs with enums like AppMsg, LayoutMsg, EditorMsg
  - Update handlers are in src/update/ (separate files for different concerns)
  - Mouse/keyboard input handling is in src/runtime/app.rs and src/runtime/input.rs
  - The layout system uses LayoutNode tree with SplitContainer and EditorGroup nodes in src/model/editor_area.rs
  - Already have clap for CLI argument parsing (startup config handled in src/cli.rs)
  - Already have native file dialogs via rfd crate

- The editor already supports:
  - Opening files via CLI: `red file.txt`
  - File dialogs (Cmd+O Open File, Shift+Cmd+O Open Folder)
  - Tab management and split views
  - workspace_root field exists on AppModel but is not fully utilized yet

next lets focus on implementing the remaining features in @docs/feature/workspace-management.md

   if root.is_dir() {\n            roots = Self::scan_directory(root, 0)?;\n            Self::sort_nodes(&mut roots);\n        }\n\n        Ok(Self { roots })\n    }\n\n    /// Scan a directory return Ok(Vec::new());\n        }\n\n        let mut nodes = Vec::new();\n\n        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir)? {\n            let entry = entry?;\n            let path = entry.path();\n\n dir_node = FileNode::new_dir(path.clone());\n                // Recursively scan children\n                dir_node.children = Self::scan_directory(&path, depth + 1)?;\n                Self::sort_nodes(&mut dir_node.children);\n                dir_node\n            } else {\n                FileNode::new_file(path)\n            };\n\n            // Skip empty directories? (optional, currently keeping them)\n            nodes.push(node);\n        }\n\n        Ok(nodes)\n    }\n\n    /// Check if a path should be ignored\n    fn should_ignore(path: &Path) -> bool {\n        let name = path\n            .file_name()\n            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())\n            .unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        // Check exact matches\n        for pattern in IGNORE_PATTERNS {\n            if pattern.starts_with(\"*.\") {\n                // Extension pattern\n                let ext = &pattern[2..];\n                if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) == Some(ext) {\n /// Sort nodes: directories first, then alphabetically (case-insensitive)\n    fn sort_nodes(nodes: &mut [FileNode]) {\n        nodes.sort_by(|a, b| {\n            match (a.is_dir, b.is_dir) {\n                (true, false) => std::cmp::Ordering::Less,\n                (false, true) => std::cmp::Ordering::Greater,\n                _ => a.name.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.name.to_lowercase()),\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    /// Refresh the file tree from disk\n    pub fn refresh(&mut self, root: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n        *self = Self::from_directory(root)?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Count total visible items (for scrolling calculations)\n    pub fn count_visible(&self, expanded: &HashSet<PathBuf>) -> usize {\n        self.roots\n            .iter()\n            .map(|node| Self::count_visible_node(node, expanded))\n            .sum()\n    }\n\n    fn count_visible_node(node: &FileNode, expanded: &HashSet<PathBuf>) -> usize {\n        let mut count = 1; // This node\n\n        if node.is_dir && expanded.contains(&node.path) {\n            for child in &node.children {\n                count += Self::count_visible_node(child, expanded);\n            }\n        }\n\n        count\n    }\n\n    /// Get the nth visible item (for hit testing)\n    pub fn get_visible_item(&self, index: usize, expanded: &HashSet<PathBuf>) -> Option<&FileNode> {\n        let mut current = 0;\n        for node in &self.roots {\n            if let Some(found) = Self::get_visible_item_node(node, index, &mut current, expanded) {\n                return Some(found);\n            }\n        }\n        None\n    }\n\n    fn get_visible_item_node<'a>(\n        node: &'a FileNode,\n        target: usize,\n        current: &mut usize,\n        expanded: &HashSet<PathBuf>,\n    ) -> Option<&'a FileNode> {\n        if *current == target {\n            return Some(node);\n        }\n        *current += 1;\n\n        if node.is_dir && expanded.contains(&node.path) {\n            for child in &node.children {\n                if let Some(found) = Self::get_visible_item_node(child, target, current, expanded) {\n                    return Some(found);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        None\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Workspace\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// Workspace state - manages file tree and sidebar\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Workspace {\n    /// Root directory of the workspace\n    pub root: PathBuf,\n\n    /// Expanded folder paths\n    pub expanded_folders: HashSet<PathBuf>,\n\n    /// Currently selected item in file tree\n    pub selected_item: Option<PathBuf>,\n\n    /// File tree cache\n    pub file_tree: FileTree,\n\n    /// Sidebar visibility\n    pub sidebar_visible: bool,\n\n    /// Sidebar width in logical pixels (scale-independent)\n    pub sidebar_width_logical: f32,\n\n    /// Scroll offset in the file tree (in items)\n    pub scroll_offset: usize,\n}\n\nimpl Workspace {\n    /// Create a new workspace from a directory\n    pub fn new(root: PathBuf, metrics: &ScaledMetrics) -> std::io::Result<Self> {\n        let file_tree = FileTree::from_directory(&root)?;\n\n        // Auto-expand the root level\n        let mut expanded_folders = HashSet::new();\n        // We don't add root itself since it's not shown as a node\n\n        Ok(Self {\n            root,\n            expanded_folders,\n            selected_item: None,\n            file_tree,\n            sidebar_visible: true,\n            sidebar_width_logical: metrics.sidebar_default_width_logical,\n            scroll_offset: 0,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Get sidebar width in physical pixels\n    pub fn sidebar_width(&self, scale_factor: f64) -> f32 {\n        self.sidebar_width_logical * scale_factor as f32\n    }\n\n    /// Set sidebar width from physical pixels\n    pub fn set_sidebar_width(&mut self, physical_width: f32, scale_factor: f64) {\n        self.sidebar_width_logical = physical_width / scale_factor as f32;\n    }\n\n    /// Toggle folder expansion\n    pub fn toggle_folder(&mut self, path: &Path) {\n        if self.expanded_folders.contains(path) {\n            self.expanded_folders.remove(path);\n        } else {\n            self.expanded_folders.insert(path.to_path_buf());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Expand a folder (no-op if already expanded)\n    pub fn expand_folder(&mut self, path: &Path) {\n        self.expanded_folders.insert(path.to_path_buf());\n    }\n\n    /// Collapse a folder (no-op if already collapsed)\n    pub fn collapse_folder(&mut self, path: &Path) {\n        self.expanded_folders.remove(path);\n    }\n\n    /// Check if a folder is expanded\n    pub fn is_expanded(&self, path: &Path) -> bool {\n        self.expanded_folders.contains(path)\n    }\n\n    /// Refresh the file tree from disk\n    pub fn refresh(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {\n        self.file_tree.refresh(&self.root)\n    }\n\n    /// Get visible item count (for scrollbar)\n    pub fn visible_item_count(&self) -> usize {\n        self.file_tree.count_visible(&self.expanded_folders)\n    }\n\n    /// Get the depth of a path relative to workspace root\n    pub fn depth(&self, path: &Path) -> usize {\n        path.strip_prefix(&self.root)\n            .map(|rel| rel.components().count())\n            .unwrap_or(0)\n    }\n\n    /// Reveal a file in the tree (expand parent folders and select)\n    pub fn reveal_file(&mut self, path: &Path) {\n        // Expand all parent folders\n        let mut current = path.parent();\n        while let Some(parent) = current {\n            if parent.starts_with(&self.root) && parent != self.root {\n                self.expand_folder(parent);\n            }\n            current = parent.parent();\n        }\n\n        // Select the file\n        self.selected_item = Some(path.to_path_buf());\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tests\n// assert_eq!(\n            FileExtension::from_path(Path::new(\"main.rs\")),\n            FileExtension::Rust\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            FileExtension::from_path(Path::new(\"app.tsx\")),\n            FileExtension::TypeScript\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            FileExtension::from_path(Path::new(\"Cargo.lock\")),\n            FileExtension::Lock\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            FileExtension::from_path(Path::new(\".gitignore\")),\n            FileExtension::Git\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_file_tree_sorting() {\n        let mut nodes = vec![\n            FileNode::new_file(PathBuf::from(\"zebra.txt\")),\n            FileNode::new_dir(PathBuf::from(\"alpha\")),\n            FileNode::new_file(PathBuf::from(\"apple.txt\")),\n            FileNode::new_dir(PathBuf::from(\"beta\")),\n        ];\n\n        FileTree::sort_nodes(&mut nodes);\n\n        // Directories should come first\n        assert!(nodes[0].is_dir);\n        assert!(nodes[1].is_dir);\n        assert!(!nodes[2].is_dir);\n        assert!(!nodes[3].is_dir);\n\n        // Within each group, should be alphabetical\n        assert_eq!(nodes[0].name, \"alpha\");\n        assert_eq!(nodes[1].name, \"beta\");\n        assert_eq!(nodes[2].name, \"apple.txt\");\n        assert_eq!(nodes[3].name, \"zebra.txt\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_ignore_patterns() {\n        assert!(FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\".git\")));\n        assert!(FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\"node_modules\")));\n        assert!(FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\"target\")));\n        assert!(FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\".DS_Store\")));\n        assert!(!FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\".gitignore\")));\n        assert!(!FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\"src\")));\n        assert!(!FileTree::should_ignore(Path::new(\"main.rs\")));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_workspace_folder_toggle() {\n        let metrics = ScaledMetrics::new(1.0);\n        let mut ws = Workspace {\n            root: PathBuf::from(\"/test\"),\n            expanded_folders: HashSet::new(),\n            selected_item: None,\n            file_tree: FileTree::default(),\n            sidebar_visible: true,\n            sidebar_width_logical: metrics.sidebar_default_width_logical,\n            scroll_offset: 0,\n        };\n\n        let folder = Path::new(\"/test/src\");\n\n }\n}\n"

super::layout::update_layout;\n\n/// Handle workspace messages (file tree, sidebar)\npub fn update_workspace(model: &mut AppModel, msg: WorkspaceMsg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n    match msg {\n        WorkspaceMsg::ToggleSidebar => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                workspace.sidebar_visible = !workspace.sidebar_visible;\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::ToggleFolder(path) => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                workspace.toggle_folder(&path);\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::ExpandFolder(path) => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                workspace.expand_folder(&path);\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::CollapseFolder(path) => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                workspace.collapse_folder(&path);\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::SelectItem(path) => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                workspace.selected_item = Some(path);\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::SelectPrevious => {\n            select_adjacent_item(model, -1);\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::SelectNext => {\n            select_adjacent_item(model, 1);\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::OpenFile { path, preview: _ } => {\n            // For now, always open as a permanent tab\n            // TODO: Implement preview tab behavior\n            update_layout(model, LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(path))\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::RevealActiveFile => {\n            reveal_active_file(model);\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::StartSidebarResize { initial_x: _ } => {\n            // Store initial resize state in UiState if needed\n            // For now, we handle resize directly\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::UpdateSidebarResize { x } => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                let scale_factor = model.metrics.scale_factor;\n                let min_width = workspace.sidebar_width(scale_factor).max(\n                    model.metrics.sidebar_min_width_logical * scale_factor as f32,\n                );\n                let max_width = model.metrics.sidebar_max_width_logical * scale_factor as f32;\n\n                let new_width = (x as f32).clamp(min_width, max_width);\n                workspace.set_sidebar_width(new_width, scale_factor);\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::EndSidebarResize => Some(Cmd::Redraw),\n\n        WorkspaceMsg::Refresh => {\n            if let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace {\n                if let Err(e) = workspace.refresh() {\n                    model.ui.set_status(format!(\"Failed to refresh: {}\", e));\n                } else {\n                    model.ui.set_status(\"File tree refreshed\");\n                }\n            }\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Select adjacent item in the file tree\nfn select_adjacent_item(model: &mut AppModel, delta: i32) {\n    let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace else {\n        return;\n    };\n\n    let visible_count = workspace.visible_item_count();\n    if visible_count == 0 {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Find current selection index\n    let current_index = if let Some(selected) = &workspace.selected_item {\n        find_visible_index(&workspace.file_tree, selected, &workspace.expanded_folders)\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n\n    let new_index = match current_index {\n        Some(idx) => {\n            let new_idx = idx as i32 + delta;\n            new_idx.clamp(0, visible_count as i32 - 1) as usize\n        }\n        None => {\n            // No selection, select first or last based on direction\n            if delta > 0 {\n                0\n            } else {\n                visible_count.saturating_sub(1)\n            }\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Get the item at new index\n    if let Some(node) = workspace\n        .file_tree\n        .get_visible_item(new_index, &workspace.expanded_folders)\n    {\n        workspace.selected_item = Some(node.path.clone());\n    }\n}\n\n/// Find the visible index of a path in the file tree\nfn find_visible_index(\n    tree: &crate::model::FileTree,\n    target: &PathBuf,\n    expanded: &std::collections::HashSet<PathBuf>,\n) -> Option<usize> {\n    let mut current = 0;\n    for node in &tree.roots {\n        if let Some(idx) = find_visible_index_node(node, target, &mut current, expanded) {\n            return Some(idx);\n        }\n    }\n    None\n}\n\nfn find_visible_index_node(\n    node: &crate::model::FileNode,\n    target: &PathBuf,\n    current: &mut usize,\n    expanded: &std::collections::HashSet<PathBuf>,\n) -> Option<usize> {\n    if &node.path == target {\n        return Some(*current);\n    }\n    *current += 1;\n\n    if node.is_dir && expanded.contains(&node.path) {\n        for child in &node.children {\n            if let Some(idx) = find_visible_index_node(child, target, current, expanded) {\n                return Some(idx);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    None\n}\n\n/// Reveal the currently active file in the tree\nfn reveal_active_file(model: &mut AppModel) {\n    // Get active file path\n    let active_path = model\n        .editor_area\n        .focused_document()\n        .and_then(|doc| doc.file_path.clone());\n\n    let Some(path) = active_path else {\n        model.ui.set_status(\"No file to reveal\");\n        return;\n    };\n\n    let Some(workspace) = &mut model.workspace else {\n        model.ui.set_status(\"No workspace open\");\n        return;\n    };\n\n    // Check if file is within workspace\n    if !path.starts_with(&workspace.root) {\n        model\n            .ui\n            .set_status(\"File is outside workspace\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    workspace.reveal_file(&path);\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::{Document, EditorArea, EditorState, ScaledMetrics, Workspace};\n\n    fn test_workspace() -> Workspace {\n        let metrics = ScaledMetrics::new(1.0);\n        Workspace {\n            root: PathBuf::from(\"/test\"),\n            expanded_folders: std::collections::HashSet::new(),\n            selected_item: None,\n            file_tree: crate::model::FileTree::default(),\n            sidebar_visible: true,\n            sidebar_width_logical: metrics.sidebar_default_width_logical,\n            scroll_offset: 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_toggle_sidebar() {\n        let mut model = AppModel::new(800, 600, 1.0, vec![]);\n        model.workspace = Some(test_workspace());\n\n        assert!(model.workspace.as_ref().unwrap().sidebar_visible);\n        update_workspace(&mut model, WorkspaceMsg::ToggleSidebar);\n        assert!(!model.workspace.as_ref().unwrap().sidebar_visible);\n        update_workspace(&mut model, WorkspaceMsg::ToggleSidebar);\n        assert!(model.workspace.as_ref().unwrap().sidebar_visible);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_toggle_folder() {\n        let mut model = AppModel::new(800, 600, 1.0, vec![]);\n        model.workspace = Some(test_workspace());\n\n        let folder = PathBuf::from(\"/test/src\");\n        assert!(!model\n            .workspace\n            .as_ref()\n            .unwrap()\n            .is_expanded(&folder));\n\n        update_workspace(&mut model, WorkspaceMsg::ToggleFolder(folder.clone()));\n        assert!(model\n            .workspace\n            .as_ref()\n            .unwrap()\n            .is_expanded(&folder));\n\n        update_workspace(&mut model, WorkspaceMsg::ToggleFolder(folder.clone()));\n        assert!(!model\n            .workspace\n            .as_ref()\n            .unwrap()\n            .is_expanded(&folder));\n    }\n}\n"
}

  update_traced(model, msg)\n40:     }\n41:     #[cfg(not(debug_assertions))]\n42:     {\n43:         update_inner(model, msg)\n44:     }\n45: }\n46: \n47: /// Inner update logic (no tracing)\n48: fn update_inner(model: &mut AppModel, msg: Msg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n49:     let result = match msg {\n50:         Msg::Editor(m) => {\n51:             // When in CSV mode, intercept navigation messages and route to CSV\n52:             let csv_info = model.editor_area.focused_editor().and_then(|e| {\n53:                 e.view_mode.as_csv().map(|csv| (true, csv.is_editing()))\n54:             });\n55: \n56:             if let Some((true, is_editing)) = csv_info {\n57:                 if let Some(csv_msg) = map_editor_to_csv(&m, is_editing) {\n58:                     return csv::update_csv(model, editor::update_editor(model, m)\n64:         }\n65:         Msg::Document(m) => {\n66:             // When in CSV mode, intercept document messages for cell editing\n67:             let csv_info =                 // Block other document messages in CSV mode\n76:                 return None;\n77:             }\n78:             document::update_document(model, m)\n79:         }\n80:         Msg::Ui(m) => ui::update_ui(model, m),\n81:         Msg::Layout(m) => layout::update_layout(model, m),\n82:         Msg::App(m) => app::update_app(model, m),\n83:         Msg::Syntax(m) => syntax::update_syntax(model, m),\n84:         Msg::Csv(m) => csv::update_csv(model, m),\n85:     };\n86: \n87:     sync_status_bar(model);\n88:     result\n89: }\n90: \n91: /// Map text editor movement messages to CSV navigation messages\n92: ///\n93: /// When not editing: arrows move cell selection\n94: /// When editing: left/right move cursor in cell, up/down confirm and navigate\n95: fn map_editor_to_csv(editor_msg: &EditorMsg, is_editing: bool) -> Option<CsvMsg> {\n96:     match (editor_msg, is_editing) {\n97:         // When editing, left/right move cursor within cell\n98:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Left), true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorLeft),\n99:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Right), true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorRight),\n100:         // When editing, up/down confirm edit and navigate\n101:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Up), true) => Some(CsvMsg::ConfirmEditUp),\n102:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Down), true) => Some(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit),\n103:         // When editing, Home/End move cursor within cell\n104:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStart, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorHome),\n105:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineEnd, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditCursorEnd),\n106: \n107:         // When not editing, standard cell navigation\n108:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Up), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveUp),\n109:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Down), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveDown),\n110:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Left), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveLeft),\n111:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Right), false) => Some(CsvMsg::MoveRight),\n112:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStart, false) => Some(CsvMsg::RowStart),\n113:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineEnd, false) => Some(CsvMsg::RowEnd),\n114:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorDocumentStart, _) => Some(CsvMsg::FirstCell),\n115:         (EditorMsg::MoveCursorDocumentEnd, _) => Some(CsvMsg::LastCell),\n116:         (EditorMsg::PageUp, _) => Some(CsvMsg::PageUp),\n117:         (EditorMsg::PageDown, _) => Some(CsvMsg::PageDown),\n118:         _ => None,\n119:     }\n120: }\n121: \n122: /// Map document messages to CSV cell editing messages\n123: ///\n124: /// When not editing: InsertNewline starts editing, InsertChar starts with that char\n125: /// When editing: InsertNewline confirms edit, InsertChar inserts into buffer\n126: fn map_document_to_csv(doc_msg: &DocumentMsg, is_editing: bool) -> Option<CsvMsg> {\n127:     match (doc_msg, is_editing) {\n128:         (DocumentMsg::InsertNewline, false) => Some(CsvMsg::StartEditing),\n129:         (DocumentMsg::InsertNewline, true) => Some(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit),\n130:         (DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch), false) => Some(CsvMsg::StartEditingWithChar(*ch)),\n131:         (DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch), true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditInsertChar(*ch)),\n132:         (DocumentMsg::DeleteBackward, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditDeleteBackward),\n133:         (DocumentMsg::DeleteForward, true) => Some(CsvMsg::EditDeleteForward),\n134:         _ => None,\n135:     }\n136: }\n137: \n138: /// Traced update wrapper (debug builds only)\n139: ///\n140: /// Captures before/after cursor state and logs diffs for debugging.\n141: /// Filters out noisy messages like BlinkCursor from logging.\n142: #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n143: fn update_traced(model: &mut AppModel, msg: Msg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n144:     use crate::messages::UiMsg;\n145: \n146:     // Skip logging for noisy periodic messages\n147:     let is_noisy = matches!(&msg, Msg::Ui(UiMsg::BlinkCursor));\n148: \n149:     let msg_name = msg_type_name(&msg);\n150:     let _span = if is_noisy {\n151:         None\n152:     } else {\n153:         Some(span!(Level::DEBUG, \"update\", msg = %msg_name).entered())\n154:     };\n155: \n156:     let before = model.focused_editor().map(CursorSnapshot::from_editor);\n157: \n158:     if !is_noisy {\n159:         debug!(target: \"message\", msg = %msg_name, \"processing\");\n160:     }\n161: \n162:     let result = update_inner(model, msg.clone());\n163: \n164:     let diff = if let (Some(ref before), Some(editor)) = (&before, model.focused_editor()) {\n165:         let after = CursorSnapshot::from_editor(editor);\n166:         let d = before.diff(&after);\n167:         if let Some(ref diff) = d {\n168:             debug!(target: \"cursor\", %diff, \"state changed\");\n169:         }\n170:         d\n171:     } else {\n172:         None\n173:     };\n174: \n175:     if let Some(editor) = model.focused_editor() {\n176:         editor.assert_invariants_with_context(&msg_name);\n177:     }\n178: \n179:     if let Some(ref mut overlay) = model.debug_overlay {\n180:         overlay.record_message(msg_name.clone(), diff);\n181:     }\n182: \n183:     result\n184: }\n185: \n186: /// Get a display name for a message type\n187: ///\n188: /// Uses Debug formatting to include variant names and arguments.\n189: /// Example outputs:\n190: /// - `Editor::MoveCursor(Up)`\n191: /// - `Document::InsertChar('x')`\n192: /// - `App::Resize(1920, 1080)`\n193: #[cfg(debug_assertions)]\n194: fn msg_type_name(msg: &Msg) -> String {\n195:     match msg {\n196:         Msg::Editor(m) => format!(\"Editor::{:?}\", m),\n197:         Msg::Document(m) => format!(\"Document::{:?}\", m),\n198:         Msg::Ui(m) => format!(\"Ui::{:?}\", m),\n199:         Msg::Layout(m) => format!(\"Layout::{:?}\", m),\n200:         Msg::App(m) => format!(\"App::{:?}\", m),\n201:         Msg::Syntax(m) => ## Assistant

All phases complete. Let me provide a summary:

### Summary

Successfully implemented Phases 0, 2, and 3 of the workspace management feature:

#### Phase 0: ScaledMetrics Extension
Added file tree constants to [src/model/mod.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/model/mod.rs#L251-L266):
- `file_tree_row_height` (22px base, scales with DPI)
- `file_tree_indent` (16px base)
- `sidebar_default_width_logical` (250px, scale-independent)
- `sidebar_min_width_logical` / `sidebar_max_width_logical` (150px / 500px)
- `resize_handle_zone` (4px base)

#### Phase 2: Workspace Data Structures
Created [src/model/workspace.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/model/workspace.rs) with:
- `FileExtension` enum - classifies files by type (Rust, JS, Python, etc.) with icons
- `FileNode` - tree node representing file or directory
- `FileTree` - recursive tree with directory scanning, ignore patterns, sorting
- `Workspace` - manages root, expanded folders, selection, sidebar state

Integrated into `AppModel` by replacing `workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>` with `workspace: Option<Workspace>`.

#### Phase 3: Messages and Update Logic
Added `WorkspaceMsg` enum to [src/messages.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/messages.rs#L403-L449):
- `ToggleSidebar`, `ToggleFolder`, `ExpandFolder`, `CollapseFolder`
- `SelectItem`, `SelectPrevious`, `SelectNext`
- `OpenFile { path, preview }`, `RevealActiveFile`
- `StartSidebarResize`, `UpdateSidebarResize`, `EndSidebarResize`, `Refresh`

Created [src/update/workspace.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update/workspace.rs) handler.

#### Next Steps (Phases 4-8)
- **Phase 4**: Sidebar rendering (add theme colors, render file tree)
- **Phase 5**: Mouse interaction (hit testing, click handling)
- **Phase 6**: Keyboard navigation (Cmd+B toggle, arrow keys)
- **Phase 7**: File system watching (notify crate)
- **Phase 8**: Tab integration (wire OpenFile to EditorArea)

Can be loaded from keymap.yaml at project root, or falls back to hardcoded defaults.\n5: \n6: use super::binding::Keybinding;\n7: use super::command::Command;\n8: use super::config::load_keymap_file;\n9: use super::types::{KeyCode, Keystroke, Modifiers};\n10: \n11: /// Default keymap YAML embedded at compile time\n12: const DEFAULT_KEYMAP_YAML: &str = include_str!(\"../../keymap.yaml\");\n13: \n14: /// Get the default keymap YAML content (for copying to user config)\n15: pub fn get_default_keymap_yaml() -> &'static str {\n16:     DEFAULT_KEYMAP_YAML\n17: }\n18: \n19: /// Load and merge keymaps: defaults + user overrides\n20: ///\n21: /// Loading order (each layer overrides the previous):\n22: /// 1. Embedded default keymap (compiled into binary)\n23: /// 2. User config at ~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml\n24: ///\n25: /// User bindings with `command: Unbound` will remove matching default bindings.\n26: pub fn load_default_keymap() -> Vec<Keybinding> {\n27:     // Load base defaults from embedded YAML\n28:     let mut bindings = match super::config::parse_keymap_yaml(DEFAULT_KEYMAP_YAML) {\n29:         Ok(b) => {\n30:             tracing::info!(\"Loaded embedded default keymap ({} bindings)\", b.len());\n31:             b\n32:         }\n33:         Err(e) => {\n34:             tracing::warn!(\n35:                 \"Failed to parse embedded keymap: {}, using hardcoded defaults\",\n36:                 e\n37:             );\n38:             default_bindings()\n39:         }\n40:     };\n41: \n42:     // Try loading user config\n43:     if let Some(user_path) = crate::config_paths::keymap_file() {\n44:         if user_path.exists() {\n45:             match load_keymap_file(&user_path) {\n46:                 Ok(user_bindings) => {\n47:                     tracing::info!(\n48:                         \"Merging user keymap from {} ({} bindings)\",\n49:                         user_path.display(),\n50:                         user_bindings.len()\n51:                     );\n52:                     bindings = merge_bindings(bindings, user_bindings);\n53:                 }\n54:                 Err(e) => {\n55:                     tracing::warn!(\n56:                         \"Failed to load user keymap from {}: {}\",\n57:                         user_path.display(),\n58:                         e\n59:                     );\n60:                 }\n61:             }\n62:         }\n63:     }\n64: \n65:     bindings\n66: }\n67: \n68: /// Merge user bindings into base bindings\n69: ///\n70: /// User bindings override or extend base bindings:\n71: /// - If user binding has same keystroke + conditions → replaces base\n72: /// - If user binding command is `Unbound` → removes matching base bindings\n73: /// - Otherwise → user binding is added\n74: pub fn merge_bindings(base: Vec<Keybinding>, user: Vec<Keybinding>) -> Vec<Keybinding> {\n75:     let mut result = base;\n76: \n77:     for user_binding in user {\n78:         // Handle Unbound: remove any matching base bindings\n79:         if user_binding.command == Command::Unbound {\n80:             result.retain(|b| {\n81:                 // Keep bindings that don't match the user's keystroke\n82:                 b.keystrokes != user_binding.keystrokes\n83:             });\n84:             continue;\n85:         }\n86: \n87:         // Check if this overrides an existing binding\n88:         let existing_idx = result\n89:             .iter()\n90:             .position(|b| b.keystrokes == user_binding.keystrokes && b.when == user_binding.when);\n91: \n92:         if let Some(idx) = existing_idx {\n93:             // Replace existing binding\n94:             result[idx] = user_binding;\n95:         } else {\n96:             // Add new binding\n97:             result.push(user_binding);\n98:         }\n99:     }\n100: \n[... omitted lines 101 to 301 ...]",

 let folder_icon_color = theme.folder_icon.to_argb_u32();\n+\n+        let mut y = 0usize;\n+        let mut visible_index = 0usize;\n+\n+        // Helper closure to render a tree node recursively\n+ of visible area\n+            if *y >= sidebar_height {\n+                return;\n+            }\n+\n+            let x_offset = (depth as f32 * indent) as usize + 8; // 8px left padding\n+\n+ ## Assistant

### Summary

I've implemented the core workspace management features:

#### Completed:
1. **Makefile**: Added `make workspace` command to open `samples/syntax` as workspace
2. **Keybinding**: Bound **Cmd+1** to toggle sidebar and **Cmd+Shift+R** to reveal in sidebar
3. **Phase 0**: Extended `ScaledMetrics` with file tree constants (row height, indent, sidebar widths)
4. **Phase 2**: Created [src/model/workspace.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/model/workspace.rs) with `Workspace`, `FileTree`, `FileNode`, `FileExtension`
5. **Phase 3**: Added `WorkspaceMsg` enum and [src/update/workspace.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update/workspace.rs) handler
6. **Phase 4**: Added `SidebarTheme` and implemented `render_sidebar()` in [src/view/mod.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/view/mod.rs#L346-L493)
7. **Phase 5**: Added sidebar click handling in [src/runtime/app.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/runtime/app.rs#L519-L556) for folder toggle and file open

#### To test:
```bash
make workspace
```
Then press **Cmd+1** to toggle the sidebar, click folders to expand/collapse, click files to open them.

#### Remaining (deferred to later):
- Sidebar resize drag
- Arrow key navigation in file tree
- File system watching (Phase 7)
- Tab integration for preview tabs (Phase 8)
